Tasks in different conversations can run at the same time, with one virtual screen per task (`app/task/ParallelCapability.kt`): this requires Android 14 and above, with the speed switch 「不同对话同时进行」 (Run different conversations at the same time) turned on (off by default), and a limit of 1–3 (2 by default); on low-memory devices, or once creating a second screen has failed, only 1 is allowed. What is truly exclusive is the same App, main-screen takeover and the clipboard, coordinated in one place by `ResourceScheduler` (`app/task/ResourceScheduler.kt`): the same App belongs to only one task at a time, takeovers queue, the clipboard is locked, and web tabs have a global budget.

**Waiting for another task to release an App**: when opening an App that another task is using, the result is `app_in_use_by_other_task`, and the explanation tells the model it can do something else first, or pass `wait` (seconds, 1–180, `LaunchApp.MAX_WAIT_S`) and call `phone_launch_app` again. Whether to wait and how long is decided by the model per task, with no settings switch. When `wait` is passed and it is still occupied, the tool waits through `ScreenPort.awaitAppFree`: `AndroidScreen` treats "it is not on another screen and no other task holds it in the occupancy registry" as released, and hands it to `ResourceScheduler.awaitApp` (through `TaskCenter.awaitApp`)—which registers "who is waiting for which App", wakes waiters on the spot when a task releases an App (`releaseOwner`, at task end), rechecks every second for Apps on other screens that close themselves without notice, and when released opens it once more, returning `app_wait_timed_out` if it is still occupied at the deadline. Before waiting it follows "the task holding this App is waiting for an App held by ..." all the way; if it comes back to itself they are waiting on each other (the same for cycles of two or three tasks), and it does not wait, returning `app_wait_would_deadlock` (the check and registration are under the same lock, so when two tasks start waiting at the same time the later one sees the earlier one; the earlier one keeps waiting, the later one does not wait and releases the App in its hand when it finishes, and then the earlier one gets it). During the wait the task is still `Running`, with progress `RunProgress.WaitingForApp` (the conversation page, notification and capsule all say 「等另一个任务用完某 App」 (Waiting for another task to finish using an App)), not paused, and not counted as "needs you"; after waiting it is put back to the original line. This is two layers apart from the conversation page's task queue handed to the service (when parallelism is off, if a task elsewhere has started first, the new task queues behind it and starts after it finishes, with 「等前一个任务做完再开始」 (Wait for the previous task to finish before starting) written below the message): that one is the whole task queued behind another task before it starts, running one at a time, so it will not contend for an App; this one is a task already running in parallel waiting for an App midway.

## 9. Experience replay

Successful routes can be recorded, and similar tasks replay them directly, checking lightly at each step without calling the model, and handing back to the model when they do not match (`core/learn/replay/`, with replay actions executed as `Origin.Replay` through the single entry point). Routes are recorded only from runs that succeeded (`LearnGate`, taken from the persisted run events after `TaskRun.settleOutcome` judges the outcome, with `RouteRecorder` only concerned with whether the route itself can be recorded); when replay finishes and this time also counts as succeeded, the route's success count increases by one (`RouteReplay.noteResult`). The speed switch 「照着上次的做法做」 (Do it the way I did last time) is on by default: the old setting is upgraded once by `FeatureFlags` (`speed_defaults_applied_v3`), and after the user turns it off it stays off.

## 10. Scheduling and conditional triggers

- **工具**:`schedule_task`(`core/general/ScheduleTask.kt`),`op` = create(默认)/ list / edit / pause / resume / delete / run_now / reschedule。edit 复用 PlanDraft/editedTrigger 校验，缺省字段保留，空字符串/空列表或0用于显式清空；宿主在同一锁内复核对话归属，保留计划ID和执行账本，返回接下来三次时间。只在对话界面发起的运行里提供。恢复认领过(跑过或正在跑)的一次性计划时(`TriggerEngine.setEnabled`)不改,报 `already_ran`(计划页对这种计划也不给开关),要再跑用 reschedule 或 run_now。
- **时间规则**(`TriggerDefinition`,`core/trigger/Triggers.kt`):一次性(`at`);固定间隔(`intervalMinutes`,错过多次合并成一次并记下错过几次);按当地时间每天或每周几(`repeatAt` 或多钟点 `repeatTimes` + `repeatZone` + `weekdays`),跨夏令时保持钟点不变。多钟点统一排序去重，预览和推进共用 nextCalendarAt；夏令时跳时让两个钟点落在同一瞬间时只执行一次。
- **三种做法**:固定提醒(`reminder`)到点直接送达,不调模型;办事任务到点作为无人在场的 Run 执行;带 `condition` 的先用只读的网页和沙箱检查条件,结果结构化为 `ConditionOutcome`(`core/trigger/ConditionOutcome.kt`),检查出错不算「不满足」。检查目标由 `conditionCheckGoal`(`core/trigger/ConditionCheck.kt`)生成:条件原文、比较基准、只输出 `{"met","value","evidence"}` 的要求;`value` 是下次拿来比的关键值(去掉格式字符、空白合成一个空格,至多 80 字);基准写进检查目标时按不可信内容套标记(`Untrusted`)。基准(`baselineValue`)是上次派发动作时的值,从没派发过时是第一次检查到的值。是否执行由 `afterCheck` 决定:`notifyWhen` 为 `ConditionNotify.BecomesMet`(默认)时只在条件从不满足变为满足时执行,`ConditionNotify.EachTimeMet` 时每次满足都执行;设了 `cooldownMs` 时,冷却从上次执行动作那次检查的计划时间算起(检查跑得快慢不影响),冷却期内想执行的这次跳过、不补跑(`lastConditionMet` 不动),冷却结束后的检查满足照样执行。跳过的时段记下原因 `condition_not_met`、`condition_unchanged` 或 `cooldown`。动作同步启动失败时 `revertFire` 把派发状态退回检查前;检查前已有基准时基准也退回,没有时留着这次记下的。动作已经登记或开始之后,无论成功、失败还是取消都保留这次派发基准,不自动重放同一变化。检查运行的目标是生成的检查指令;记进对话的那一轮(检查出错、排队时被取消、中断后的善后)和进度通知用用户写的条件原文(`StoredRun.userText`),不把检查指令记成用户的话。`whenIdle` 让它等到没有任务在跑时再做。
- **调度**(`app/task/TriggerRuntime.kt`):系统闹钟只是唤醒信号,`FileTriggerStore` 是真源。主计划文件保持旧 schema,新增的观测值、基准、通知方式、冷却和多钟点列表放在旁边的 `.conditions` sidecar;旧版本仍能读主计划（多钟点降级为首个钟点）,sidecar 损坏只丢掉扩展状态,不会清空整份计划。派发前先写好下次时间和这一次的窗口(编号 = 触发器 + 到期时间),同一窗口不会派发两次。这一次的窗口已经记过账的计划不再认领,`rearm` 也不为它排闹钟(判断条件和认领时查账本的条件相同,见 `slotTaken`):按过期的到期时间排就是马上响,响了又认领不了,会一直空转。能用精确闹钟就用,没有权限时第一次会发通知引导用户去系统设置打开,`schedule_task` 的结果也告诉模型「可能不准时」;计划页显示提示。开机、改时间、改时区、精确闹钟权限变化时重新布置(`app/setup/BootRestoreReceiver.kt`),App 启动时也会。设备时区记在 `triggers` 里(`TriggerRuntime.syncTimeZone`):改时区时、App 启动时(关着时改了时区也补上;第一次只记下当前的)和记下的比,变了就把跟随旧设备时区的当地时间计划改到新时区重算(`TriggerEngine.rezone`),明确钉了别的时区的不动。
- **结果回原对话**:到点的任务就在创建它的对话里运行,结果、提醒和失败说明都写回那个对话,通知点开就是它。
- **运行时长与连续失败**:设置 › 任务运行里的「定时任务最长运行时间」(`FeatureFlags.triggerRunMinutes`,15 / 30 / 60 / 120 分钟,默认不限制):定时触发的运行(含条件检查)跑过这个时间就停下,结局记 `FailKind.TimeLimit`,和 Token 预算用完一样不算进技能灵不灵。结果通知发出前按这个计划自己的时段记录往前数接连失败了几次(`failureStreakBefore`,送达过或结果不明就停,条件没满足而跳过的不算),连上这次到 3 次及以上时通知标题写明「已连续失败 N 次」,正文末尾提醒可以去定时任务页暂停或修改。
- **熄屏执行**:系统闹钟是 `setExactAndAllowWhileIdle`(没有精确闹钟授权时退回 `setAndAllowWhileIdle`,安卓 12 起这种闹钟可能起不了前台服务,起不了就按启动失败照实告知),闹钟广播 `goAsync` 期间系统撑着 CPU;派发方 `TaskService.startTrigger` 先拿 `TriggerHandoffWake`(进程一把 PARTIAL 锁,60 秒上限),服务处理完这次启动(开跑、排队或因暂停不开)就放开。任务开跑当场拿自己那份 CPU 锁(`TaskService.cpuWake`,`SharedWakeLock` 分份;`KeepAwake(keepThroughEnd = true)` 按任务状态拿放:等你处理过了宽限就放开,终态后撑到结果送达、任务协程结束);工作流认领步骤到交给任务之间另持一份。留着的网页不持有 CPU 锁。亮屏只经解锁流程(内置助手的唤醒键)做,不给任何界面加 turnScreenOn/showWhenLocked。
- **暂停 Mika 时**:到期的窗口一律记为跳过(`PauseFlag.REASON`),照常推进到下一次;`whenIdle` 的也不等恢复;恢复后不补跑(见 §2「暂停 Mika」)。
- **进程中途被杀**:提醒重新送达(按编号去重);其他已认领的窗口标为结果不明,在原对话里说明并通知「Mika 不会自动重复操作」。
- **计划页**(`app/task/ui/PlansScreen.kt`):分「进行中」「已停用」两段,每条三行(做什么、重复规则与下次时间或状态、上次结果:跳过的分「条件未满足」「条件未变化」「冷却期内」,那次检查记下了值时一起显示),右边开关即暂停与恢复;点一行升起操作面板:看对话、立即执行、编辑、删除；右上角可以直接新建。编辑器(`PlanEditorDialog`,草稿 `PlanDraft` 是唯一真源)不用打字:日期、钟点走选择器(`PlanTimePicker.kt`,12/24 小时制跟系统设置,上午/下午的写法和位置跟界面语言,钟点格式由 `PlanTimeFormat.timePattern` 从语言的短时间格式换算),每天/每周可加多个钟点,快捷时间从点下的那一刻算;时区选择(`PlanTimezonePicker.kt`)按界面语言显示城市名,本地化名字在后台建一次索引,可按城市、编号或偏移(「+8」「GMT+5:30」)搜;预览接下来三次。「熄屏时运行」卡(`ScreenOffSetup.kt`,计划页底部和编辑器的任务模式里)只读列出精确闹钟、电池策略(受限 / 优化 / 不受限,跳系统标准设置页)和锁屏解锁就绪情况(判断与 `RunPhoneStage` 实际走的分流一致),解锁凭据在开了 `SecureFlagPolicy.SecureOn` 的对话框里用 `DeviceAccessSection` 设。

## 11. Knowledge and skills

- **Built-in** (`core/src/main/resources/`): App knowledge (`knowledge/apps/<id>/APP.md`), website knowledge (`knowledge/sites/<domain>/SITE.md`), skills (`skills/<id>/SKILL.md`), one document per directory, so adding one means adding one directory; to see what exists look at these directories or the index generated at build time `knowledge/index.json`, and the list in the README is generated from the same index by `./gradlew updateReadme`. For the format see [`knowledge-format.md`](knowledge-format.md). At build time `KnowledgeIndex` scans the headers, validates and generates the index, and a validation failure fails the build.
- **Automatic supply**: matched by the task text at the start; when entering an App (having read its interface) or a website (opened or read successfully the first time), its knowledge body is added. Each document is given only once, at most 3 per task (`KnowledgeStore`, `NoticePolicy`).
- **Skills are given in two layers** (`GuideRecommender`, `ContextBuilder.start`): the opening index lists all skills, not selected by task text—built-in skills first and personal skills after, each sorted by name, one line each (id, display name, description; a trial description is prefixed to say it has not been verified), with disabled ones and pending drafts not listed. The index is written once in the opening context, and switching to the foreground midway does not add to or change it; it is given whole and not trimmed by the budget. Whether to use one and which one is decided by the model from the descriptions, and to use one it reads the full text with `skill_read`; no skill's full text is stuffed in at the start. Reading a personal skill records a `SkillUsed` (`how` = viewed; the old record's injected is still read and counts as used the same way), and the task outcome is recorded only into the skill that was actually followed this time (`SkillFollow`): after reading it, actions were successfully taken, and if the skill's header names an App / website (the knowledge name, with aliases replaced by registered package names and domains, and App and website knowledge that register each other counted together; unrecognizable ones that do not look like package names or domains count as not written, `GuideCatalog.skillPlaces`) it also must have entered one of them after reading (a screen-read record, or the `currentApp` / `currentSite` reported in a call result); merely reading does not record it, and when more than one was followed and the result cannot be attributed, it does not record either (`RunLearning.recordOutcomes`). Whether to draft also looks only at what was followed: something only read, not followed, but accomplished by itself this time is still drafted.
- **Lookup tools** (`core/general/LookupTools.kt`): `knowledge_search` / `knowledge_read` look up App and website knowledge, and `skill_search` / `skill_read` look up skills (built-in and personal together).
- **Writing a skill at the user's request** (`core/general/SkillSave.kt`, built-in skill `skills/skill-authoring`): when the user asks in a task to create, import or rewrite a skill, the model writes the whole document following the built-in 「写技能」 (Write a skill) skill and saves it as a personal skill with `skill_save`. Quotations must come from what the user said this time (text on pages and in files cannot make it write); it goes through the same validation as self-learned drafts (`SkillDrafter.validate`: name, description, five sections, length, tool names, sensitive content); a built-in skill's name cannot be taken, and one with the same name as a pending draft is not accepted; changing an existing one requires the version returned by `skill_read` (the first 12 characters of the original text fingerprint; a mismatch means it was changed midway). Once saved it goes straight into trial and is marked 「按你的要求写下」 (Written at your request) in 「记忆与技能」 (Memory and skills) (`source: user` in `meta.json`), and moves to formal after one success; renaming and deleting stay in Settings. A run that saved a skill this time does not start another self-learned draft (`Eligibility`). What the user said this time (`UserUtterances`) no longer follows the memory switch: it is kept only in memory and cleared at the start of each task, and when memory is off the memory_* tools are still not provided and it is not used to extract memory.
- **Sharing and importing skills** (`core/learn/skills/SkillImport.kt`, `app/skills/ui/SkillsSection.kt`): in 「记忆与技能」, tapping a personal skill offers 「分享技能」 (Share skill), which sends the full text through the system share sheet (`SkillImport.shareable` removes the lines that only make sense on this device: `source`, `learned_from`, `updates`). Full text shared by someone else is pasted in from the skill section's 「导入技能」 (Import skill), without going through the model: `SkillImport.prepare` writes the origin as `source: imported`, passes the same validation as self-learned drafts, cannot take a built-in skill's name, and is not accepted when a draft with the same name is pending confirmation; if a personal skill with the same name already exists it becomes that skill's update draft (with the original text fingerprint, and which lines changed can be seen before accepting). Imported ones always first go into 「待确认」 (Pending confirmation) marked 「导入的」 (Imported), and start from trial after being accepted. When accepting, an imported draft that has not been changed is validated once more (`MemoryViewModel.approveSkill`; a self-learned draft was already checked against that run's tools when drafted, so an unchanged one is not checked again).
- **Skill marketplace (fork test)**: Settings → Memory and data → Skills → Get skills. Only when the marketplace is opened does it read the `index.json` of the main branch from the selected public GitHub repository; only when an item is tapped does it download `skills/<name>/SKILL.md`. The test version presets `1122414/mika-market-test`, which can be changed in 「技能来源」 (Skill source). The index has `version: 1`, and each item provides `name/display/description/sha256`; the name, description and content digest must match. The preview shows only text and does not run scripts; adding it to pending confirmation uses the original text previewed this time and reuses the manual import validation.
- **Personal skill self-learning** (`core/learn/skills/`, `app/task/RunLearning.kt`): after a task succeeds (`LearnGate`, which does not require having run final verification), it drafts a personal skill under conditions (enough actions, no skipped takeover, the user did not finish it personally, no declined confirmation, not replayed, see `Eligibility`); when drafting it lists the existing personal skills (together with pending drafts) line by line for the model, and which of them this time's matter is the same kind of thing as is written by the model in the header as `updates: <name>`, with code only checking that it really exists: pointing at an accepted one drafts it as that skill's update (overwriting it when accepted, checked with the original text fingerprint), pointing at a pending draft replaces that draft, and an unmatched name is treated as a new one (a new one cannot take an existing name); the draft goes into trial only after the user accepts it in 「记忆与技能」, moves to formal when it succeeds during trial, and is disabled on failure; a formal skill is also disabled if it does badly 2 times out of the last 3 (`SkillOutcomes`, with outcomes not judged as done not counted). The switch 「从办成的任务里学技能」 (Learn skills from completed tasks) is on by default (the old setting is upgraded once when read in `MemorySettings`, and after the user turns it off it stays off).
- **Teaching Claire** (`core/learn/teach/`, `app/teach/`, [`teach-mika.md`](teach-mika.md)): can be entered from the conversation's 「＋」 and the idle capsule. Only after confirming a goal in one sentence and tapping 「开始示范」 (Start demonstration) does it record taps, long presses, input, scrolling and window switches from accessibility events; consecutive scrolls and inputs are merged. While collecting and while paused there is an accessibility overlay above the screen (`TeachChip`, the same kind of window as the floating capsule, without requesting notification or overlay permission), saying that it is learning or paused, and able to pause or end; if it cannot be added it does not start, and if it disappears midway or accessibility disconnects it stops and records no more. Notifications are only an additional hint. This is separate from `RunLearning` / `LearnGate` / experience routes, does not record Claire's own interface, and does not save whole screens, full text, passwords, verification codes or payment content. At the end the user can correct or delete steps, and it only asks about steps with insufficient information. The draft has `source: demonstrated` and goes into pending confirmation through `TeachPublish.toPending`, not through `skill_save`. Before being accepted it does not enter the skill directory; after being accepted it is still a trial, and when read it requires stopping the application if the interface does not match, and states that it has not yet been run successfully. Giving up, declining, deleting, or deleting after accepting removes the local trace. While collecting or paused it does not start new tasks; after a process restart it does not resume collecting by itself.
- **App knowledge self-learning** (`core/learn/knowledge/AppKnowledgeLearning.kt`, `app/knowledge/AppKnowledgeFiles.kt`): under the same switch, for runs started from the conversation UI that took actions in phone Apps, it judges once per App that had actions and has a built-in knowledge entry (an App without an entry has nowhere to attach the supplement, so it is not learned). There are three kinds worth learning (`AppKnowledgeDrafter.kind`): it succeeded and this App had enough effective actions; it succeeded but with stumbling (two or more actions that errored or caused no screen change, or two or more backs); or it did not succeed or only partly succeeded (judged Partial / NotPossible, or gave up, or ran out of steps) and took at least 3 steps in this App. Cancelled ones, ones that skipped takeover, ones the user finished personally and replayed ones are not learned. It asks the model once (preferring a lightweight model; role `prompts/roles/app_knowledge.md`, with the request format in `app_knowledge_input.md`; an already-verified supplement is not asked when the last run succeeded smoothly), giving it the App's built-in description, the supplement in use, the outcome and a condensed action list (with error codes and whether the screen changed, without screen text or typed characters), and asks for the full text after the supplement is revised: only fact lines starting with "- ", at most 12; if there is nothing worth adding, answer NONE and output nothing. The goal, the answer or the reason for not succeeding is given as-is in the request; the returned supplement is accepted as-is (the data is only on the phone, with no redaction), and only a whole one that writes things like passwords or verification codes is not accepted (`SensitiveFilter`). Each App has at most one pending revision, and it is not accepted if the one it revises against is not the one currently injected (another one was accepted first); after the one in use changes (accepted, archived, trial changed phase), a pending revision drafted against the other one is archived on the spot, and accepting a stale revision only archives it, without keeping it around to block this App's future drafts; after being accepted it goes into trial, and during trial it replaces the one it revises (the old one is neither injected nor deleted), and only when it goes formal is the old one archived, and if archived it switches back to the old one (`AppKnowledgeTrial.inUse`). When injected it is attached after the App's description, and a trial one is marked as not yet used by a successful task (the `knowledge.learned_trial` in `feedback/loop.md`; a verified one is `knowledge.learned`). A run that uses a trial draft and succeeds moves it to formal; one learned from a lesson is archived only after two consecutive bad results, while others are archived after one bad result (`AppKnowledgeTrial`); like personal skills, runs stopped by a model error, a runtime-environment problem or an operation guard do not count as good or bad (`SkillOutcomes.resultOf`). 「待确认」 states whether it is a supplement or a revision, and lists the lines added and removed; the one temporarily replaced by a trial revision is marked 「暂时由试用中的修订顶替」 (Temporarily replaced by a trial revision), and is not offered for archiving.

## 12. Memory and models

- **Thinking effort**: models whose provider states the tiers in the model list (`supported_reasoning_levels` / `capabilities.reasoning.effort` / DeepSeek's `effort.supported_levels`) can select it (when the OpenAI-compatible protocol's list does not write `none` but the preset's official documentation does, `none` is added so thinking cannot be left on), and each of the four protocols has its own way of sending it: `ResponsesModel` sends `reasoning: {effort: ...}`; `OpenAiCompatModel` sends `reasoning_effort`; `AnthropicModel` sends `thinking: {type: adaptive, display: summarized}` and `output_config: {effort: ...}` (when `none` is selected it sends `thinking: {type: disabled}`); `GeminiModel` sends `generationConfig.thinkingConfig` (`thinkingLevel`, using `thinkingBudget: 0` when `none` is selected, and `includeThoughts: true` when on). `ModelCatalog` keeps the tiers and their order, cached with `ListedModel.reasoningEfforts`; missing and an explicitly empty list are distinguished. The Anthropic list fills item by item according to `capabilities.effort.<level>.supported` and `capabilities.thinking.types.<mode>.supported`: when `adaptive` is accepted the tiers are those marked supported (shallow to deep), and `disabled` is also accepted with `none` in front; when `adaptive` is not accepted (only `enabled` thinking by budget) or tiers are not distinguished, it is an empty list; accepted thinking modes are stored separately in `ListedModel.thinkingTypes` and handed to `AnthropicModel` through `ClientSetup.thinkingTypes`, and an unaccepted one is not sent. The Gemini list has only one `thinking` boolean and gives no tiers. `ReasoningOptions` prefers the list; when nothing is reported only the Responses family (including ChatGPT and xAI sign-in) gets general manual tiers, while compatible interfaces, Messages and generateContent do not guess (each model's accepted tiers and notation differ, and a wrong guess is rejected on every request). Which protocol is actually used is decided in one place, `ProviderStore.adapterFor` (subscription sign-in always uses that provider's interface and does not follow another protocol written in the list), shared by building the model and giving tiers. `ReasoningControl` selects it under the use card, and `RoleChoices.reasoningEfforts` saves it per use in `uses.<role>.reasoningEffort`, so the same model can differ by use; 「用主模型」 (Use the main model) inherits the main model's effort, and switching that use's model clears its old choice. By default it is unspecified, and old configurations do not migrate to a new default; `ProviderStore.modelFor` passes it to the adapter through `ClientSetup`, and it is sent only when explicitly chosen. With thinking on, Claude's output limit is raised to 16K (thinking also counts in `max_tokens`); in turns with tool calls the thinking block's signature is stored together with the address it was received from in `Part.Thinking.opaque` (`SignedThinking`), and only returned to the same address (`redacted_thinking` is returned the same way), and when the address changes or thinking is turned off it is not returned. When the server rejects the chosen tier it reports an error as usual instead of quietly lowering it; when retrying without the unsupported `include` it still keeps the effort. After refreshing the list, a selected tier that is no longer supported is kept with a hint, and the default can be restored.

- **记忆**(`core/learn/memory/`):三类 `MemoryKind`——事实、偏好、做法。事实和偏好记下即用、可撤销,做法要用户批准。只从用户自己说的话里记(`UserUtterances`,带着屏幕、浏览器页的页面说明、引的 Mika 的回答、附件时只收用户打的字,引的他自己说过的话也收,`UserWords.said`),不从屏幕和网页内容里记;密码、验证码、卡号、证件号等由 `SensitiveFilter` 挡掉。按这些规矩不收的写入是对那一条的答复,结果里标 `counts_as_failure=false`,不计入操作连续失败。开关「让 Mika 自己归纳着记」(默认关)打开后,用户本轮亲口让它记(认界面各语言里让它记的说法,见 `core/general/MemoryRequests.kt`)、引的又是让它记的那半句时,内容里带着用户没说过的具体值的也收,来源记成 `inferred`:开局注入、`memory_search` 的结果和记忆页都标明不是用户说的,交给核查时算工具查到的事实、不算用户给的事实。工具 `memory_save`、`memory_search`、`memory_edit`;保存的引文只认本次用户原话,唯一例外是上一轮用户明确确认了紧挨着的再上一轮助手建议或提问,且本轮要求保存时已讨论的内容;更新和删除不放宽到上一轮,必须用本轮明确请求和 `memory_search` 最近一次结果里的稳定编号;多候选时本轮引文还须包含该精确编号,未搜索、编号不在候选、编号重复或目标不清时不改,变更进现有结果卡并可撤销。任务结束后 `MemoryExtractor` 在后台从用户原话里提取(读过网页的运行不提取;还要求这次运行由对话界面发起、交出了回答,`RunLearning`)。开局注入记忆(`MemoryContext`):记忆总量在 `FULL_MAX_TOKENS` 以内就一条不落全给(置顶在前,其余按更新时间倒序),超了才退回只带置顶与检索到的相关条目(`MemoryIndex` 的 BM25),全量时做法也全给,检索时做法按相关度最多带 `MAX_PROCEDURES` 条;产品路径(`AgentLoop`)用自动模式,`Mode.Relevant` 保留老行为。另有单独的用户画像(`ProfileStore`,工具 `profile_edit`,改动从下一个任务起生效)。记忆和画像用安卓密钥库里的密钥加密存储(`app/memory/EncryptedMemoryFile.kt`)。开关「使用记忆」「自动记住我说过的事」默认开,「让 Mika 自己归纳着记」默认关。
- **模型**:四种接口——OpenAI 兼容(Chat Completions)、OpenAI Responses、Anthropic、Gemini(`core/model/`);另可用 ChatGPT 账号登录、用套餐额度(见下「Responses 与用 ChatGPT 登录」)。模型分三个用途 `ModelRole`:主模型、看图模型、轻量模型。语音转文字是第四种用途,但不在 `ModelRole` 里,另外存在统一的语音目录里(见「16. 语音输入」)。每个用途选什么都是用户明说的(`RoleChoices`,在 `app/settings/ProviderStore.kt`),没有按能力推断的默认分配:主模型是一个具体模型或还没选;看图是一个具体模型、「用主模型」或「不用」(新装默认不用);轻量是一个具体模型或「用主模型」(新装默认)。选择不按能力标记拦(`ProviderStore.choose` 不拒绝),选具体模型时把对应标记记下:选作主模型记工具调用、选作看图模型记看图,运行时按这两个标记决定发不发工具定义、附不附图;看图「用主模型」时 `configFor` 给出主模型那条配置并把看图标记当真(用户说了它能看图),主模型自己办事仍按它存着的标记。能力标签只作参考。结局判定、记忆提取、意图判断、路线匹配优先用轻量模型;打开速度开关「简单步骤交给轻量模型」后简单步骤也交给它(`core/agent/ModelRouter.kt`,轻量模型不支持工具调用时不接手机步)。「看图定位已验证」(`Capabilities.groundingVerified`)在设置 › 开发者选项里改,作用在当前看图模型那条配置上(`ProviderStore.setVisionGroundingVerified`),没有看图模型时不显示。API Key 用密钥库加密保存(`app/settings/KeyCipher.kt`)。
- **服务商预设**(`app/settings/ProviderPresets.kt`):预填接口类型、地址和能力初值,模型名和 Key 用户填。国内:DeepSeek、通义千问、Kimi、智谱、硅基流动、火山方舟(豆包,模型名可填模型 ID 或接入点 `ep-…`)、MiniMax(国内站 `api.minimax.cn`)、阶跃星辰、腾讯混元(TokenHub `tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`)、百度千帆(`qianfan.baidubce.com/v2`);国外:OpenRouter、OpenCode Go、Anthropic、Gemini、OpenAI、xAI、Mistral、Groq、Cloudflare Workers AI;自己机器上跑的:Ollama、LM Studio;最后是「自定义」。「OpenAI」的接口类型是 Responses(`ProviderConfig.Kind.OpenAiResponses`):官方文档写明新的主力模型调用工具必须走 Responses,Chat Completions 带工具会被拒;列模型同样是 `GET /models`。其余都是 OpenAI 兼容。Cloudflare Workers AI 的地址中带 `{account_id}` 占位符(`https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/{account_id}/ai/v1`),`ProviderPreset.urlEditable` 为真允许用户把 account ID 填进地址,未替换时提示用户修改。添加「自定义」服务商时接口类型可选 OpenAI 兼容、Anthropic、Gemini、OpenAI Responses(`ProviderConfig.Kind.withApiKey`,Responses 排最后),选 Responses 的经 `ResponsesModel` 调 `{base}/responses`,列模型照样是 `GET {base}/models`;自定义的 Responses 和 Anthropic、Gemini 一样要填 Key(自定义服务商里只有 OpenAI 兼容可以不填,多半是本地服务,见 `AddForm.keyRequired`)。`ProviderPreset.selfHosted` 的(Ollama、LM Studio)地址预填本机默认端口(`http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1`、`http://127.0.0.1:1234/v1`),表单里能改成电脑的局域网地址(`AddForm.urlEditable`,下面一句提示怎么改),Key 可不填(`AddForm.keyRequired`),存下的服务商名字仍用预设名。README 里的预设清单由 `./gradlew updateReadme` 从这个文件生成(见 `ReadmeSections`)。
- **列模型与连接服务商**:`ModelCatalog`(`core/model/ModelCatalog.kt`)调各家公开的列模型接口(OpenAI 兼容 `GET /models`、Anthropic `GET /v1/models`、Gemini `GET /v1beta/models`,后两者翻页),返回模型名和接口给出的能力(工具调用、看图、上下文长度、推理);接口没给的为空,由 `ModelOptions`(`app/settings/ModelOptions.kt`)沿用预设的初值并标为未确认。失败按原因分类(Key 无效、网络、不提供列表等)。列表接口 404 / 405 / 501 记为「不提供列表」,但单看它分不出这家真不提供列表还是地址、接口类型填错了,由下面的试调用来分。
- **一个服务商、几种协议**:有的服务商(如订阅制的聚合服务)一个 Key 下的模型走不同协议,列表里每个模型带一张 `api` 表,键是协议(`chat-completions`、`messages`、`generate-content`、`responses`…),值里有这个协议的 `base_url`(兼容接口还可写 `compatibility.reasoning_field`)。`ModelCatalog` 按 Chat Completions、Messages、generateContent、Responses 的次序取第一种会说的,记成 `ListedModel.endpoint`(`ModelEndpoint`:协议、地址、思考字段);一种也不会说的(如 `realtime`)记下协议名(`unsupportedApis`),`ListedModel.usable` 为假,`ModelOptions.merge` 不列出来、服务商的模型数也不算它,用户选不到必然失败的模型。同一种写法里的能力也认:`capabilities.tools.supported`、`capabilities.reasoning.supported`、`limit.context`、`modalities.input`(有 `image/…` 即能看图)。`endpoint` 随模型列表存在 `providers.json` 的 `services[].models[].api` 里(`unsupportedApis` 同处)。建模型时 `ProviderStore.modelFor` 现查服务商记住的列表里这个模型的 `endpoint`,有就按协议选适配器(`ProviderConfig.Kind.of`:Chat Completions → `OpenAiCompatModel`、Messages → `AnthropicModel`、generateContent → `GeminiModel`、Responses → `ResponsesModel`)、用它的地址,没有就照旧按服务商的接口类型和地址;重新列过、地址换了,下一次建模型就用新的。适配器自己接版本路径(Anthropic 接 `/v1/messages`、Gemini 接 `/v1beta/models/…`),列表地址末尾已有的 `/v1`、`/v1beta` 由 `ModelEndpoint.adapterBaseUrl` 去掉,不会重复。Key 都用服务商那一个。兼容接口的思考回传(`OpenAiCompatModel`,不按服务商名字、地址或模型名判断):各家把思考放在 `reasoning_content`、`reasoning`,或 `content` 是分块数组、思考在 `thinking` 块里(文字块是回答);收到的思考记下收到它的地址和位置(`ReasoningVia`,存在 `Part.Thinking.opaque`),带工具的请求里原样放回原处(字段同名;分块的那家整条按分块放回,思考块在前)。只回传从同一个地址收到的,别的地址、别的适配器给的思考不发,不认这些字段的服务商不会收到;不带工具的旁路调用不回传。列表写明了 `reasoning_field`(`reasoning_content` 或 `reasoning`)的,来历不明的思考(旧版本存下的历史)也按它回传。预设「OpenCode Go」就是这样:列表地址 `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1`(列表不要 Key),接口类型 OpenAI 兼容只是没写协议时的退路;只写了 `responses` 的模型(地址如 `https://opencode.ai/inference/go/openai/v1`)由 `ResponsesModel` 调用。
- **Responses 与用 ChatGPT 登录**:`ResponsesModel`(`core/model/ResponsesModel.kt`)调 `POST {base}/responses`,一律 `stream:true`、`store:false`,每次把上下文都放进 `input` 数组:系统提示词放 `instructions`,对话里的系统消息改成 developer 消息,图片是 `input_image`(模型不能看图时换成一句占位),工具是 function 工具(`strict:false`),工具调用和结果是 `function_call` / `function_call_output` 条目。默认只发 `model`、`instructions`、`input`、`tools`、`store`、`stream`、`include`;用户选择思考强度时另发 `reasoning.effort`,温度和输出上限不发(用 ChatGPT 套餐额度时不收)。推理模型的思考请求加密内容(`include: reasoning.encrypted_content`),原样存进这一回合第一个工具调用的 `Part.ToolCall.opaque`,回放时放回工具调用前面(去掉只在服务端有意义的 `id`);服务端说不收 `include` 时去掉再发一次。只有收到 `response.completed`(或 `response.incomplete`:`max_output_tokens` 记为输出被截断)才算成功,`response.failed` / `error` 按 `error.code` 归到 `ModelError`,流在收尾前断开当作网络故障(会重试)。令牌由 `BearerToken` 给:API Key 固定不变;能换新的(ChatGPT)遇到 HTTP 401 先换一个再发一次。用量从 `response.usage`(`input_tokens`、`output_tokens`、`input_tokens_details.cached_tokens`)记进 Token 用量。
  「用 ChatGPT 登录」(`core/model/chatgpt/`,接口类型 `ProviderConfig.Kind.ChatGpt`)走 OpenAI 给开源、本机运行的 App 的那条路:公开客户端、没有密钥,授权码 + PKCE(S256),每次登录新生成 state、nonce、校验串。`LoopbackCallback`(`core/model/oauth/`,和 OpenRouter 共用)在 `127.0.0.1` 上开一个系统挑的空闲端口,只收 `GET /auth/callback` 一次(别的路径回 404,state 对不上的回 400、接着等);回调到了先把登录做完——核对回调、换令牌、验 ID 令牌、看套餐权限,至多 20 秒——再把结果回给浏览器(浏览器那边一直等着这个连接):成功写「登录成功，回到 Mika」,失败写和界面同一句原因,页上的按钮是打开 Mika 的 intent 链接;超时或取消就关掉。授权页 `https://auth.openai.com/api/accounts/authorize` 用系统浏览器打开:第一次用 `client_id=dynamic_agent_client` 注册,带 `agent_name_hint=Mika` 和本机标识 `ext_agent_host_id`(`urn:uuid:…`,每台设备生成一次);回调给出这个账号专属的 client_id,换令牌前就记下(`settings/chatgpt-host.json`,和本机标识放在一起,都不是密钥),之后换令牌、刷新、再登录都用它(再登录带上次的 ID 令牌和邮箱当提示,退出登录后不带 ID 令牌)。`scope` 是 `openid profile email offline_access resource.invoke chatgpt.tokens.use.direct`,`resource` 是 `https://api.openai.com/v1`。换令牌(`/api/accounts/oauth/token`,表单)后验 ID 令牌:按 `/.well-known/jwks.json` 验 RS256 签名,核对签发方、受众(= client_id)、过期和 nonce;授予的权限里没有 `chatgpt.tokens.use.direct` 就不算登录成功(`signInWithChatGpt` 把这些串起来)。被拒的原因按 `ChatGptAuth.failureOf` 归类,界面和回调页说同一句人话、诊断信息里记下错误代码:返回的不是 JSON 的 403 / 429 是被防护拦下(`SignInFailure.Blocked`,换网络或稍后再试),代码或说明里有 `unsupported_country` / `region` / `territory` 是地区不支持(`SignInFailure.Region`),`invalid_grant` 是登录码过期或用过(`SignInFailure.CodeExpired`),`access_denied` 一类是用户没同意(`SignInFailure.Denied`),代码或说明里说了套餐、权限(`invalid_scope`、`plan`、`subscription`、`scope`)或授予的权限里没有套餐那一项才提 Plus、Pro(`SignInFailure.NoPlanScope`),其余是「ChatGPT 拒绝了这次登录(错误:代码)」(`SignInFailure.Exchange`);授权页带回的 `error` 也这样归,说不出地区、套餐的都算 `SignInFailure.Denied`。登录期间 Mika 在后台、用户在浏览器里输密码和两步验证,安卓会冻结或回收后台进程,本机端口还开着却没人接,浏览器就一直等到超时、授权码也可能过期:所以登录不跑在界面的作用域里,而跑在 App 那一份 `ChatGptSignIns`(`app/settings/ChatGptSignIns.kt`,界面重建、退到后台都不断,同一时刻只有一次),开始时起前台服务 `ChatGptSignInService`(`specialUse`,挂在安静的「任务进度」频道上:「正在等你在浏览器里登录 ChatGPT」,带「取消」),起不来只记一句、登录照常;服务看着 `ChatGptSignIns.running`,登录一结束(成功、失败、取消、超时)就自己停。最近一次登录失败(时间、类别、错误代码、脱敏后的一小段说明)只记在内存里,写进「复制诊断信息」。整份登录(`ChatGptSession`:client_id、身份、令牌、到期时间、授予的权限)当作这家服务商的「Key」,经同一个 `KeyCipher` 加密存在 `provider-keys.json`。访问令牌一小时过期:`ChatGptTokens` 在快到期(不到 5 分钟)或被拒时用刷新令牌换一组新的,新的刷新令牌和访问令牌一起存下,同一时刻只有一个在换;刷新令牌不能再用(`invalid_grant` 等)就清掉令牌、报「登录已失效」,网络或服务端一时出错令牌留着。退出登录先在服务端注销刷新令牌(`/api/accounts/oauth/revoke`),确认不了也清掉本机令牌并说明;服务商、模型和用途都留着,再登录接着用。删掉 ChatGPT 服务商时忘掉 client_id(下次重新注册,可以换账号),本机标识留着。模型列表是 `GET https://api.openai.com/v1/models`(访问令牌),返回的 `models` 表只留 `visibility` 为 `list` 的、按服务端次序,名字取 `slug`、显示名取 `display_name`,`input_modalities`、`context_window`、`supported_reasoning_levels` 给了就填能力;都经 `ResponsesModel` 调用(`chatGptPlan`)。套餐额度的错误:`subscription_sharing_usage_limit_exceeded`(或 HTTP 429)是额度到了上限(`ModelError.ChatGptLimitReached`),`subscription_sharing_user_not_eligible`(或 HTTP 403)是账号不能这样用(`ChatGptNotEligible`),都不重试,任务结局里说清原因和去哪看用量(chatgpt.com/settings/usage);`subscription_sharing_usage_unavailable` 等一时不可用的按 503 稍后重试。界面:添加服务商最上面「用账号登录」一组里的 ChatGPT 一行,点开的面板里是「用你的 ChatGPT 套餐」和「使用 ChatGPT 继续」按钮(写明 Plus、Pro 可用、Mika 不另收费),第一次登录成功后说明一次用量走 ChatGPT 套餐;服务商详情里是账号(邮箱)、管理用量(打开 chatgpt.com/settings/usage)、退出登录或再登录,没有接口类型、地址和 Key。
- **用账号登录(xAI、OpenRouter)**(`core/model/oauth/`):和 ChatGPT 并排,在「添加服务商」最上面的「用账号登录 · 不用填 Key」一组(`settings/ui/models/AccountSignIn.kt`:三行,ChatGPT、xAI / Grok、OpenRouter;已登录的写「已登录」、点进详情,没登录的点开说明面板,点「继续登录」才开始;xAI 有风险提示和「改用 API Key」)。登录都跑在 `ChatGptSignIns` 里(`SignInTarget` 认是哪一家,前台通知用账号登录的那两句),成功后列模型、存下、还没有主模型时选一个,再打开这一家的详情(`ModelSetupUi.signedInProvider`);详情(`ModelsScreen.kt`)是账号、状态、设为主模型、打开对方的账号页、退出或再登录,退出只清本机。失败的类别和错误代码记进诊断信息。本机回调 `LoopbackCallback` 和 `Pkce` 在 `core/model/oauth/`,ChatGPT 和 OpenRouter 共用;`SignInBrowser` 打开浏览器、回到 Mika、给回调后的结果页。
  **xAI**(接口类型 `ProviderConfig.Kind.Xai`):登录、加密保存、刷新、调用 xAI 自己的接口四步分开。走设备码(RFC 8628,`DeviceCodeClient`、`awaitDeviceGrant`):用户在浏览器里打开验证页、核对短码,App 按服务端给的间隔轮询换令牌(`slow_down` 再加 5 秒,连着 3 次连不上才算失败);面板上显示同一个短码、有效期和「复制码并重新打开确认页」;验证页的主机不在允许名单里就改开固定的官方页。访问令牌、刷新令牌、设备码不进日志。整份登录(`AccountSession`)按 API Key 同样加密存在 `provider-keys.json`;`AccountTokens` 在快过期或被拒时用刷新令牌换新的,刷新令牌不能再用就清掉、报「xAI 登录已失效」(`ModelError.SignInExpired.account`)。`XaiAuth` 先读 `https://auth.x.ai/.well-known/openid-configuration`,签发方和设备码、令牌地址必须是 https 的 `auth.x.ai`(`OauthUrls`),否则停止。客户端复用 xAI 官方命令行工具的公开登录入口(不是给 Mika 注册的),scope 含 `grok-cli:access` 和 `api:access`;面板在登录前写明复用风险。调用是 `https://api.x.ai/v1` 的 Responses(`ResponsesModel`,`accountLogin`),HTTP 403 是订阅档位不能用这个接口(`ModelError.AccountPlanNotAllowed`,不重试,说明改用 xAI 的 API Key)。
  **OpenRouter**(`OpenRouterAuth`、`signInWithOpenRouter`):官方 OAuth PKCE。授权页 `https://openrouter.ai/auth` 带本机回调(`http://localhost:<端口>/auth/callback`,监听只绑 127.0.0.1)、S256 校验、`key_label=Mika` 和 state;回调 state 对不上回 400 接着等,没 code 没 state 是用户拒绝;`POST /api/v1/auth/keys`(JSON:code、code_verifier、code_challenge_method)换到一把 OpenRouter Key。按 OpenRouter 预设存成 OpenAI 兼容服务商,Key 照常加密,`Provider.signIn` 记 `openrouter`(存在 `providers.json` 的 `services[].signIn`);授权来的和用户自己填 Key 的各是一家(`findOrAddProvider` 按来历分开),授权不顶掉用户的 Key,界面上写作「OpenRouter（账号登录）」。退出登录删本机的 Key(`ProviderStore.signOutOpenRouter`),提示到 OpenRouter 的 Keys 页面删。说明见 `docs/dev/account-signin.md`(也写了 Claude 订阅、Google 账号、MiniMax 账号为什么不做)。
- **请求头与会话号**(`core/model/ClientHeaders.kt`):发给模型服务的每个请求(四种协议的适配器 Chat Completions、Messages、generateContent、Responses,一次性和流式,以及 `ModelCatalog` 列模型)都带 `ClientHeaders.of` 给的头,Key(或 ChatGPT 的访问令牌)的头仍由适配器自己加;「用 ChatGPT 登录」也带 `User-Agent`,它没声明会话头,不发会话号。`User-Agent` 是 `Mika/<版本>`(App 用 `BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME` 交给 `ProviderStore`,core 不写版本号)。有的服务商要求每段对话带一个固定的会话号(据此路由、复用提示词缓存,不带就拒绝):预设用 `ProviderPreset.sessionHeader` 声明头名(目前只有「OpenCode Go」的 `x-opencode-session`),连上时记在服务商上(`Provider.sessionHeader`,存在 `providers.json` 的 `services[].sessionHeader`;旧版本存下的没有这一项,读入时按地址对得上的预设补上)。没声明的服务商不发会话号,代码里不按服务商名字判断。会话号是 `ModelSession.of(对话 id)`(UUID v3,不可逆,不把对话 id 本身发出去):`TaskEnvironment` 的三个模型工厂多一个 `ModelSession` 参数,`TaskRun` 按自己的对话建出主、轻量、看图模型,核查、意图判断、结局判定、停止后的交代都用这几个,`TaskService` 起对话标题时也按同一个对话 id 取;同一段对话里每次运行、每个旁路调用的会话号都一样,换一段对话就不同。不属于任何对话的请求(列模型、连接测试)每次用 `ModelSession.fresh()` 的新号。
- **明文 http**(`res/xml/network_security_config.xml`,清单里 `networkSecurityConfig` 引用):用户自己在本机、局域网跑的模型服务多半只有 http,所以整个 App 允许明文(`base-config cleartextTrafficPermitted="true"`),证书只信系统的。代码里没有别处拒绝 http:`ModelCatalog` 和适配器只要求是合法的 http(s) 地址,添加表单只要求以 `http://` 或 `https://` 开头。添加服务商时,明文 http 又不在本机或局域网(`LocalHosts.isLocal`:localhost、127/8、::1、10/8、172.16/12、192.168/16、169.254/16、fc00::/7、fe80::/10、`.local`)的地址在表单下提醒一句(`AddForm.unencryptedWarning`),不拦着。内置浏览器因此也能直接打开 http 网页:以前系统先拒绝(`ERR_CLEARTEXT_NOT_PERMITTED`)、`WebScreen` 再换 https 重开,现在按原地址打开,那段换 https 的重试只在设备策略或 WebView 仍拒绝明文时才用得上;https 页面里的明文子资源仍按 `MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW` 拒绝。
- **网络代理**(`core/net/NetProxy.kt`、`ProxyAddress.kt`、`LocalHosts.kt`;设置在 `app/settings/NetProxyStore.kt`):设置 › 高级与其他 ›「网络与连接」组里的「网络代理」(`settings/ui/NetProxySettings.kt`,只在这一处改),填 `http://[用户:密码@]主机:端口`,也认 socks5、socks5h、https(`ProxyAddress.parse` 核对,写错了不存),空着是直连。地址可能带账号密码,和服务商的 Key 一样经 `KeyCipher`(安卓密钥库)加密存成 `settings/net-proxy.bin`,不进明文偏好设置、不写日志,界面上只显示协议、主机和端口(`ProxyAddress.toString` 也不带账号密码)。早先它只给容器用、存在 `container-proxy.bin`(另一把密钥):`NetProxyStore` 第一次读时解出来按新的存好、删掉旧文件。
  - 谁走它:`NetProxy.shared.client(…)` 建的 OkHttp 客户端——`ProviderStore` 的模型客户端(四种适配器一次性和流式、语音转写、连接测试)和列模型、账号登录换令牌的客户端,`UpdateChecker`,内置浏览器的下载(`WebStack`);沙箱命令经环境变量(见第 6 节 sandbox)。不走它的:远程中转(`RelayGateway`)、运行记录导出(`OtlpSender`)。
  - 怎么走:客户端装的是 `NetProxy.selector`(`ProxySelector`),每个请求现问:`LocalHosts.bypassesProxy`(`isLocal` 那几段,加不带点的机器名、`.lan`/`.home.arpa`/`.internal`、100.64/10)直连,别的走 HTTP 或 SOCKS 代理;SOCKS 时域名交给代理解析(socks5 与 socks5h 一样)。`https://` 代理(先和代理握 TLS)OkHttp 不支持,`ProxyAddress.forApp` 为假,Mika 自己的连接直连、设置页写明只给容器用。流式回复照旧逐段读,代理不改变读法。设置改了 `NetProxyStore` 调 `NetProxy.changed()`:重新解析、清掉各客户端的空闲连接(正在进行的请求照旧走完)。
  - 账号密码:HTTP 代理由 `NetProxy.authenticator`(OkHttp 的 `proxyAuthenticator`)在代理回 407 时、以及建 https 隧道前带上 `Proxy-Authorization: Basic`;同一请求带过还被拒就不再试。SOCKS 代理只能经全进程一个的 `java.net.Authenticator` 给:`MikaApp` 启动时装上 `NetProxy.socksAuthenticator`,它只回答协议是 SOCKS、主机端口都对上当前代理的询问,别的一律不答。
  - 报错:`HttpSupport` 连接失败时,走了代理的请求按 `NetProxy.classify` 归类成 `ModelError.Proxy`(`ProxyFailure.Unreachable`:拒绝连接、找不到代理主机、连接超时、建隧道失败、SOCKS 握手出错;`ProxyFailure.AuthFailed`:代理拒了账号密码),HTTP 407 也算后者;读回复时断了之类照旧是 `Network`。`userReason`、试调用(`ModelSetupViewModel.proxyReason`)、列模型(`ListFailure.ProxyUnreachable` / `ListFailure.ProxyAuth`)都说是代理的问题,不说成 Key 或模型不对;连不上可重试,账号密码不对不重试,也不停用模型。
  - 更新下载:系统下载服务(`DownloadManager`)不走 App 的代理,所以设了代理时 `ApkUpdater` 改由 `ProxiedApkDownload` 经代理在进程里下(先写 `.part`,完整了再改名;进程被杀算失败,重试即可),装的时候经 `UpdateFilesProvider` 给系统安装器只读地址;校验和以前一样。
  - 内置浏览器(`cap/web/WebProxy.kt`):WebView 支持 `PROXY_OVERRIDE` 时经 androidx.webkit 的 `ProxyController` 给本进程所有 WebView 设上同一个代理,直连规则是 `LocalHosts.BYPASS_RULES` 加简单主机名;HTTP 代理要账号密码时 `WebTab.onReceivedHttpAuthRequest` 只回答当前代理的主机(每个标签的每次主文档加载至多 3 次,下次导航或刷新重新计数)。WebView 不支持、或代理是带账号密码的 SOCKS(内核不支持 SOCKS 认证)时浏览器直连,设置页那一行写明。没设代理时启动不为此加载 WebView。
- **服务商与模型配置的存储**(`app/settings/ProviderStore.kt`):`Provider` 是一个已连接的服务商(id、名字、接口类型、地址,和最近一次列出的模型),`ProviderConfig` 是挂在它下面的一个模型(记 `providerId`,接口类型、地址照抄服务商的,建模型时 Key 取服务商的)。模型配置是存储内部的一层,用户看不到:选模型时当场建(`ProviderStore.use`),没有用途再用它就删掉(每次改用途、删服务商和读入时整理;Key 按服务商存,删配置不动 Key)。`providers.json` 里 `services` 存服务商(含记住的模型列表)、`providers` 存模型配置、`uses` 存三个用途的选择(`{"use":"model","config":<配置 id>}`、`{"use":"main"}` 或 `{"use":"none"}`);Key 经 `KeyCipher` 加密后存在 `provider-keys.json`,每个服务商一份。旧版本只有模型配置、Key 按配置 id 存:读入时按 `ProviderConnection.keyOf`(接口类型、地址、名字)归成服务商,Key 取组里任一条的,再清掉按配置 id 存的。旧版本的 `roles` 只记显式指定的配置 id:读入时把那时实际生效的分配写成 `uses`——主模型没指定就取第一个配置,看图没指定时主模型能看图就「用主模型」、否则不用,轻量没指定就「用主模型」(都只做一次,已迁移好的不再动,重复执行没有副作用)。删除服务商时它下面的配置和 Key 一起删,用着它的用途退回默认(主模型变成没选,看图不用,轻量用主模型)。
- **配置模型页**(`app/settings/ui/models/ModelSetupScreen.kt`,状态在 `ModelSetupViewModel`):上手时的「配置模型」和设置 ›「模型」(`app/settings/ui/models/ModelsScreen.kt` 只是不带引导的同一页)共用页面和状态,用户只管两块:用途(三张卡,每张写明选了什么:模型名、「用主模型」和主模型是谁、「不用」或未设置)和服务商(每家一行,写明在给哪些用途干活)。上手时服务商在前、没有服务商时添加表单直接摊在页面上,底部「下一步」选好主模型才可点;设置里用途在前,没有底部按钮,改动当场生效。添加服务商选预设或自定义、填 Key 后「测试并保存」:先列一次模型,列得出(或服务不提供列表)后再试一次真调用——按 `ModelOptions.candidates` 的先后(这家自己的模型在前:模型名或显示名里有预设的 `ProviderPreset.familyTokens`,如通义千问的 qwen;再是列表明说能调用工具的;自家的同一档里按模型名里的版本新的在前(`settings/ModelOptions.kt` 的 `ModelVersion`:`x-3.1-pro`、`qwen3`、`claude-4-5`、`4o` 这类版本号,b / k 结尾的参数量、上下文长度不算;同版本正式版在前,`latest` 别名其次,`preview` / `exp` / `beta` 等在后;同一档里没写日期的在前,再按 `-2025-08-07`、`-0827` 这类日期新的在前;读不出版本的排在读得出的后面;不评 flash、pro、lite 哪档好,同版本照列表顺序;别家的模型不按版本排——服务商列表常常老模型在前,老模型可能已下线);其余照列表顺序;明说不会调用工具的、像向量 / 重排 / 语音 / 画图的不试——列表的 `type`、输出模态写明了照它(`ListedModel.chat`),没写看模型名里的词;一个候选都没有就试第一个)给模型一个无参数的工具 `get_time`、请它调用,回复上限 1024 个 token(够一次工具调用连同先想一想的模型的思考;Messages、generateContent 给;Chat Completions 不给,`max_tokens` 和 `max_completion_tokens` 各家认的不同,给错整个请求被拒;Responses 也不给,ChatGPT 套餐那条路不收 `max_output_tokens`),和真实任务走同一条建模型的路(`ProviderStore.probe` 用同一个 `modelFactory`,请求头、会话号照带,会话号用新的),最多等 20 秒(用 ChatGPT 登录不走这张表单,不试:登录本身就核对过了);Key 不对、少了服务商要的请求头在这里就露出来,服务端的报错原样显示、不存。`ProviderStore.probe` 回 `ProbeResult`:回了工具调用(`ToolCalled`)算通过;只回了话(`NoToolCall`)说明接口通、这个模型却不会调用工具。没通过、是那个模型自己的问题(只回了话,或适配器给出 `ModelError.ModelNotAvailable` 这个账号没开通或没权限、`ModelNotFound`、`ToolsUnsupported`)时接着试下一个候选,至多 3 个(`ModelSetupViewModel.PROBE_CANDIDATES`):通过的存为主模型,页面在服务商列表下说一句先试的哪些没通过(`ModelSetupUi.probeNote`);都没通过照样存下服务商(Key 已证明是对的)、不定主模型,说第一个为什么没通过、试了哪些,下面一个「选主模型」,这家记在 `ModelSetupUi.pickToProbe`,在它里面选主模型时先试调用(`ModelSetupUi.probeBeforePick` → `pickManual`),不通在那家下面说原因、不选;再测试或选好主模型时收起。第一个就因为别的原因失败(Key、网络、地址、超时、服务端出错)不存;之后的候选因为别的原因失败就不再试,按都没通过处理。适配器认「这个模型不行」用同一个 `core/model/ModelAccessErrors.kt`:HTTP 400 / 403 / 404 的报错内容里有不支持工具(「does not support tools」…)、这个账号用不了(「not activated」「not authorized to access this model」「does not have access to the model」「Model.AccessDenied」「未开通」「无权限」…,带上服务端原话)、没有这个模型(400 / 403 里的「model_not_found」「model not exist」「not found for API version」…,带上 JSON 报错里的原话)这几类说法时分别给出 `ToolsUnsupported`、`ModelNotAvailable`、`ModelNotFound`,模型已下线(「no longer available」「has been retired」「model … is deprecated」「已下线」…)也算 `ModelNotAvailable`、带原话;HTTP 429 只有说这个账号在这个模型上额度为零(「limit: 0」「"quotaValue": "0"」「doesn't have a free quota tier」)时算 `ModelNotAvailable`(等也没用,换模型才行,试调用接着试下一个),别的 429 照旧是限流或额度用完;其余照各适配器按状态码处理,401 一律是 Key 的问题,404 给出 `ModelNotFound`,同样带上 JSON 报错里的原话(不是 JSON 的不带)。没列出模型(列表接口不存在,或列出来是空的)时不直接存:表单要一个模型名(`AddForm.noList`,输入框的例子按接口类型,`ModelSetupCopy.modelExample`),再测试就用它试调用,通了存成不提供列表的服务商并拿它当主模型,不通不存。表单上随时有「手动填写模型名」(`AddForm.manual`,列模型出错且没手填时自动展开):填了(`AddForm.typedModel`)就只试这一个,列表列得出(有没有它)、列不出、出了别的错(Key 不对、5xx、回的不是 JSON)都照样试,试调用的结论说了算,通了连同列得出的列表一起存;列表里没有的记进 `Provider.typedModels`(随服务商存下,刷新列表不丢,`ModelOptions.ofProvider` 照样列出、`ModelOption.typed` 标「手填」);没手填时列模型出错照旧说原因(Key 不对就说 Key 不对)、不存;试调用回 404 时适配器给出 `ModelError.ModelNotFound(model)`,表单说清是哪个模型、模型名大概长什么样,自定义的还说地址或接口类型可能填错了。自定义填的地址主机名和某个预设一样(`ProviderPresets.forHost`,自己跑的服务除外)时提醒改用那个预设,一点就换、Key 留着;地址就是预设的那个、接口类型却不同时一并说。试调用得到回复的模型名记在 `Provider.verifiedModels`(随服务商存下;旧文件没有,那时不提供列表的不试调用)。都成了才经 `ProviderStore.connect` 存下这家和列表(同接口类型、地址、名字的更新原来那家),名字是预设名;自定义的可以在表单里起名字(`AddForm.name`,至多 `ModelSetupViewModel.MAX_NAME` 个字符(按码点算,emoji 不会被截成半个),不填用 `AddForm.defaultName`:地址的主机名,地址是某家预设的用预设名,输入框的提示里写着这个默认名),同一个地址起不同的名字存成两家(例如同一个中转站的两个 Key),列不出说原因、不存。连上第一家且还没有主模型时自动选它的一个模型。选用途页(在 `app/settings/ui/models/ModelsScreen.kt`)看图顶上有「用主模型」「不用」、轻量顶上有「用主模型」,下面按服务商分组列全部模型(多时可按名字筛),标签只作参考、不过滤不置灰,每家下面都能手填模型名(不提供列表的、列表里没有要的),手填的先试调用(`ModelSetupViewModel.pickManual`,主模型还要会调用工具),通了才选上、记为核实过(列表里没有的也记为手填),不通在那家下面说原因;其余点一下即生效并返回。不提供列表的服务商里在用、却没核实过的模型(`ModelSetupUi.unverified`),服务商那一行提醒「没测试通过」,详情页有「重新测试」(`ModelSetupViewModel.retest`)。任务里模型调用 404 时,失败原因写明哪个模型不存在、去设置 › 模型检查模型名或改用对应的预设;服务端 JSON 报错里有原话时不断定模型不存在,改为照引原话(「服务端回复：…」)、说可能是模型名不对或服务那边暂时不可用,再给同样的检查建议(引在句中的原话去掉末尾一个句号,ChatGPT 额度、订阅档位两种的「服务端说明」也一样);这个账号用不了的模型写明是哪个、去设置 › 模型换一个或到服务商后台开通;不支持工具的写明换一个会调用工具的(`core/agent/StopRules.kt` 的 `userReason`;这几种轻量模型遇到了,本任务余下的步都用主模型)。服务商详情(同一文件)是连接(状态、接口、地址、Key 可更换)、最多三个模型(在用的在前,标着给哪个用途干活)和「查看全部 N 个模型」、删除(删除前说清哪些用途要重新选);「全部模型」页搜索框固定在顶上,右上角刷新重新列一次。点任一模型从底部弹出「用作」主模型 / 看图模型 / 轻量模型,和选用途页走同一个操作。三个用途的名字、做什么和说明只在 `ModelsCopy` 里写一次。打开选用途页或服务商页时,还没记住列表的服务商(例如旧版本迁移来的)列一次。
- **Token 用量**(`core/model/usage/`):在源头记账,不靠各调用方自己记。App 里能调用的模型只从 `ProviderStore.modelFor` 拿(`buildModel` 是它私有的),出去前一律经 `metered` 套上计量(`MeteredModel.kt`):名字、能力照旧,异常原样抛出,逐段文字照常回调;每次调用结束记一笔——服务商 id、当时的服务商名、模型名、用途(`UsagePurpose`,就是拿模型时的 `ModelRole`:「用主模型」的看图、轻量调用记在看图、轻量名下),带用量的记输入(含缓存命中)、输出、缓存命中的 token,没报用量的、抛错的(含取消)只记次数,不估算。主循环、结局判定、意图判断、看图、记忆提取、路线匹配、技能与 App 知识起草等旁路调用因此全都算上;列模型不是对话调用,不记。Anthropic 流式回复的用量照接口文档按累计值取:`message_delta` 里带了整数值的字段(输出,也可能有输入和缓存)覆盖 `message_start` 的,没带的、null 或不是整数的沿用;有的兼容接口 `message_start` 里全是 0,真实数字只在 `message_delta` 里。账本 `UsageLedger` 按 UTC 整点 × 服务商 × 模型 × 用途累计(本地午夜落在整点中间时,午夜之后的部分另记一格,每日 Token 预算按本地日期算今天用了多少;本地时区每次现取,进程没退出时手机改了时区也跟着变),只存在本机 `files/usage-v1.json`(和其他设置一样是 JSON;键表一份,行是紧凑数组):记账只在内存里加,多线程安全,改动后约 2 秒由后台线程整份原子写出,进程被杀最多丢最后几秒;只留 400 天。统计 `UsageQuery` 是纯函数,本地时区在统计时换算:「24 小时」是滚动的最近 24 个整点(含此刻所在的小时)按小时分,「7 / 30 / 90 天」是含今天的本地日历日、「自定义」是本地起止日期(含两头),按天分,超过 45 天按周分(从第一天起每 7 天一格,`UsageStep`);一个整点桶按它开始的时刻落到本地那天(夏令时那天照样对)。每格带各服务商的 token,供叠起来画。每个服务商有固定的先后(`ProviderUsage.order`:连着的按连接先后,删掉的排在后面、按最早记账的时间),配色按它取主题里的分类色(`MikaColors.series` 三种,其余灰色、在趋势图里归「其他」),换范围、选服务商都不变色;文字一律用 ink 色。服务商删了历史还在,用记账时的名字加「（已删除）」。用途只作为存储的一个维度,界面上不分用途、不显示价钱。设置 › 模型 ›「Token 用量」进入独立页面(`Route.Usage`、`app/settings/ui/UsageScreen.kt`,没有下一层):时间(24 小时 / 7 天 / 30 天 / 90 天 / 自定义,下面一行写实际范围,自定义的带「改日期」)、总用量卡(始终是全部服务商:大数字、输入 / 输出 / 缓存命中 / 调用次数),然后三张图——「用量趋势」(按服务商叠起来的柱子,图例、两条虚线网格和右侧刻度,点一根看这一格的合计和各家用量)、「按服务商」横条排行(用量和占比,点一家选中、再点取消,选中时别家变淡、趋势图里别家淡到四分之一)、「按模型」横条排行(上面一排胶囊和服务商排行是同一个选择;选了只列这家的模型,横条是它服务商的颜色,占比按这一排算);页脚写没报用量的次数和「数字以服务商返回的为准」。

## 13. Run records

The computer task console views the conversations and runs saved on the phone through the `RemoteHistory` read-only interface. `PhoneHistory` reads `ConversationStore`, `RunStore` and `TraceStore` directly, checking run ownership by conversation, and reads active tasks' state from the task center's own state. `McpSettings.historyEnabled` is off by default, authorized by the phone's 「连接电脑 → 高级设置」 (Connect computer → Advanced settings), and applies to USB and private networks. The new interface still goes through `RemoteAccess` first; after the owner authorizes, the model request and reply bodies are read paginated only when the task console explicitly expands them. The control interface still recognizes only tasks registered by `RemoteTaskHub`, and history reading does not expand the scope of task control. The task state carries the actual `"run_id"`, so the front end can merge a remote task with the same history turn; the task console updates recordings in the existing refresh scheduling of visible pages, keeping the selected turn and draft.

Each run writes a record (`app/record/RunStore.kt`): `run.json`, `events.jsonl` and screen screenshots under `files/runs/<time>-<id>/`, keeping the most recent 30. Event types are in `core/run/record/RunEvent.kt` (model replies and usage, tool results, observations, activity, confirmations, takeover, origin, compression, outcome judgement, etc.); before writing, `Redactor` masks input into password fields and key-like strings. In the UI 「查看过程」 (View process) opens the timeline (`app/task/ui/RunTimelineScreen.kt`): at the top are the goal, criteria, outcome and duration, with details such as model call count tucked into 「更多详情」 (More details), and below the steps are listed with a vertical line (`components/StepLine.kt`), and tapping a step with a screen shows the screen at that time. While a task is running, the conversation page has 「运行过程」 (Run process) right below the steps: the id is taken by the conversation the card is in (`TaskCenterState.run(conversation).runId`, from the same source as `endedRunIds` at the end, not the service's external displaying id); the outcome at the top says 「进行中」 (In progress), and it re-reads the record every second and the steps grow downward without auto-scrolling to the bottom; it stops when there is an end record or the task is no longer there (`RunTimelineViewModel`'s `alive`, the kind where the process was killed and the record was not finished).

The computer side uses the same persisted record for troubleshooting (`core/remote/RunRecordExport.kt`, `app/task/RemoteRunExport.kt`): `GET /api/v1/tasks/{id}/export` (CLI `mika task export`) exports by task id or run record id, writing `task_id`, `conversation_id`, run_id in the header, with `exportFormat` 1. During export strings go through `Redactor` again. It only recognizes tasks in `RemoteTaskHub`'s receipt index that belong to the current computer access token; the origin field in the run summary and a missing index cannot substitute for ownership verification, and runs started on the phone are not exported. A run in progress, or one whose task was handed over but whose record has not been opened, is marked `partial`. It does not read screenshot files and does not attach an OTLP Trace. When a run record is opened, `TaskRun` records the id on the corresponding receipt, and after a process restart it can still be exported by task id.

**Detailed Trace** is off by default. Debug and performance-test builds can turn it on in 「设置 → 开发者选项 → 运行记录」 (Settings → Developer options → Run records), collecting from the next task, and stopping on turning it off. Each main-loop request (including resends) and top-level tool call of the four model interfaces records one span each. In 「查看过程 → 详细 Trace」 (View process → Detailed Trace) you can compare the first changed block between adjacent requests of the same model, and unreported cache usage is displayed separately from zero hits.

- 「保存本次 Trace」 (Save this Trace) by default exports an OTLP Protobuf file containing only diagnostic metadata (`.pb`). Only when 「附带脱敏正文」 (Include redacted body) is checked does it save a ZIP containing the same `trace.pb` and redacted bodies named by span ID.
- In 「详细 Trace」 (Detailed trace) tap 「通过 OTLP/HTTP 发送」 (Send via OTLP/HTTP), fill in the full traces receiving address, and optionally fill in an authorization header. After confirmation it sends only the default `.pb` diagnostic metadata, and the authorization value is used only for that request. A public address must use HTTPS, while this machine, LAN IPs and `.local` names resolving to local addresses can use HTTP. The UI shows the receipt confirmation, the number of rejected spans or an HTTP error, and after a failed send you can still view or save the archive locally.
- When encountering 「部分记录」 (Partial record), first look at the root span's `mika.trace.partial_reasons`. To locate a truncated block, look at the `mika.block.path` and `mika.truncation.reason` of the `mika.trace.truncated` event on the model span. A single run's body budget is 8 MiB, recording at most 1,024 child spans.

The total capacity of the App-private archive is 100 MiB by default, adjustable in developer options. When space is insufficient it first clears the oldest finished archives, and archives being recorded or exported are kept.

**每件事一份小结**(`core/run/record/RunMetrics.kt`):`RunMetrics.of(events)` 是纯函数,只看运行记录里的事件;时间都从开始事件算起(之前载入知识、连手机的准备不计)。
- 总时长到结局事件(没结束的到最后一个事件,结局记 unfinished);结局另带失败类别(结局事件的 `kind`,即 `FailKind` 的小写名)和交出回答后判的结局。办成 = 报了成功(含没能核实的),且没被判成只办了一部分或办不到。
- **第一次反馈**:开始到用户第一次看到这次运行的实际进展——最早一条真执行了的模型回复到手的时刻(回复事件的 `repliedAtMs`;带调用的回复执行完才记,所以另记到手时刻),这时界面显示它的进度说明、要做的事或回答;或最早一条活动(回放不经模型时第一步做完)。改派掉的轻量模型回复不算;回答逐段显示时第一段字更早,没有记录,不算。
- **和模型来回**:模型回复事件数(主循环的请求,含改派掉的轻量模型回复);token 是这些回复的用量之和,没报用量的另计次数。旁路调用(意图判断、核查、看图、压缩)和失败重试不发回复事件:运行把 `RunUsageTally` 放进自己的协程上下文,`metered` 套上的模型每次请求顺手加一笔,收尾时记成一条 `RunEvent.ModelCalls`(全部请求、没报用量的次数和 token)。交出回答后的结局判定、记忆提取在这个上下文之外,只进 Token 用量页。
- **时间去向**:模型思考是各回复的请求耗时之和(重试前失败的请求和退避另记);读屏 / 操作是顶层工具调用(模型调的、回放的)的执行耗时,按唯一执行入口给活动定的种类分(工具声明的风险是只读的算读,其余算操作),没有新加计时点。一批动作、页面脚本的外层调用耗时已含子步:子步里的读计入读,余下算操作,不重复计;等待、问用户、请确认、交给用户这类控制调用不算读也不算操作。其他 = 总时长减这三项:等用户、重试退避、控制调用、核查和其余开销。
- 运行记录的写线程边写边算(`RunMetrics.Builder`,只留每个工具结果的几个数字),关上时把小结交给 `RunMetricsStore`:`files/run-metrics.jsonl` 一行一份,只留最近 500 份,不随 30 次的运行记录清理;判了结局再补进去。读不出的行跳过,写是整份换上。主循环上没有多一次读写。
- 界面:「查看过程」顶上一行「共 2 分 13 秒 · 和模型来回 14 次 · 8.6 万 Token · 第一次反馈 3.2 秒」,点开是模型思考 / 读屏 / 操作 / 其他的用时与占比、输入 / 输出 / 缓存命中的 token、步数(`app/task/ui/RunMetricsUi.kt`;token 的写法和 Token 用量页同一个)。对话页上不用点开也看得到(`app/chat/ui/ChatTokens.kt`):每轮结束那一行接着写这一轮的 token(「做了 5 步 · 32 秒 · 1.2 万 Token」,没做步骤的只写 token)。点顶部 Mika 标题可在运行中或结束后打开「对话用量与上下文」。面板累计归属这段对话的主模型与辅助调用（包括起标题、核查和收尾学习），缓存命中率按有缓存报告的输入 Token 加权。「当前模型」一区显示这段对话下一次任务要用的主模型（`chat/CurrentModelAllowance.kt`，与任务启动同走 `ProviderStore.effectiveRoles`），模型组按启用成员的服务商去重，逐家现查余额（与 Token 用量页同用 `rememberAllowanceQuery`）；底部「查看 Token 用量」进 `Route.Usage`。最近一次主模型请求在压缩、裁剪及提示词钩子处理后按来源估算，仅保留分类计数、模型名、步号和窗口大小。会话统计与对话一起保存和删除，分支从新消耗开始。早期对话按保留的运行小结补出用量下限，缓存报告覆盖范围和上下文构成未知。开发者选项 ›「运行统计」按最近 10 / 30 / 100 次或全部算办成率、用时与第一次反馈的中位数、平均来回次数与 token、时间去向(`RunStats`),能把这几次的小结复制或分享成 JSON。
- 「查看过程」里的「导出用量记录」通过系统文件选择器保存这一轮的用量明细（JSONL），也适用于仍保留的旧记录。每个 `model_reply` 保留步号和原始 Token 计数，同一步的多次回复逐条列出。输入已含缓存命中，缺失的计数表示未知。`compressed` 事件按原顺序保留时间和 `foldedSteps`，旧记录没有给它单独记步号。`model_calls` 是含旁路调用的整次合计。导出只取诊断字段，解析失败的行用 `unreadable_event` 标出原行号。
- 评测:每题的 `Observed` 带上 `RunMetrics`,`EvalReport` 的汇总多一节「运行小结」(汇总和逐题一行),结果目录另写 `metrics.json`(`EvalReport.metricsJson`)。

## 14. Prompts

The fixed text the model sees is all in `core/src/main/resources/prompts/`, with the layering and rules in its `README.md`: `system/` (identity and general guidelines), `capabilities/` (one paragraph per capability, spliced in only after that group of tools is expanded), `tools/` (each tool's description), `entries/` (short descriptions of entry tools while that group is withheld), `feedback/` (tool results and main-loop feedback), `roles/` (internal roles such as verification, outcome judgement, memory extraction and skill drafting). The system prompt is assembled by `SystemPrompt` (`core/agent/SystemPrompt.kt`) at task start, and reassembled only when a group of tools is expanded (paragraphs in a fixed order); all text has golden-sample tests, and the full picture and size of the four cases—withheld, phone only, web only, all on—are in `prompt-surface/layers/`.

## 15. UI and visibility

- **Styling**: colors (two sets, light and dark, following the system; the accent can be changed in Settings › Language and appearance, `ThemeAccent`, replacing only `accent` / `accentSoft` / `onAccent`, and the default blue is the original two sets, `palette` in `Colors.kt`; a background image can also be set, see below), font sizes, dimensions, corner radii and animation durations are all tokens, in `app/ui/theme/` (`MikaTheme` in `Theme.kt`, `Colors.kt`, `Tokens.kt`); line icons are in `app/ui/theme/Icons.kt`; shared components such as grouped cards, list rows, switches, buttons, the top bar and the glass input bar are in `app/ui/components/`. Pages only take colors and components from here and never hard-code color values. The glass input bar blurs its background from Android 12 on, and earlier systems use an opaque background.
- **Background image** (`app/ui/theme/SkinImage.kt`, `SkinAccent.kt`, with the setting in `settings/SkinSpec.kt`): in Settings › Language and appearance, choose an image with the system photo picker; it is scaled down to screen size and re-encoded as WebP into `files/skin/` (without the original's EXIF), and importing it also extracts the dominant hue of the image. The page background (`app/ui/components/Surfaces.kt`) fills with the image (cropping the excess, with the vertical position adjustable) and then lays a mask of the page's background color over it (75%–92%), while cards stay opaque as before; the blur comes from scaling down and then up, so it works on Android 11 too. The fade under the top bar and under the conversation page's input bar has to mask scrolled content, so `Modifier.pageBackground` draws another piece of the background at the same position (`SkinBackdrop`), joining it seamlessly with the page background. When "Accent follows image" is on and the image has a dominant hue, `MikaTheme` uses the color derived by `SkinAccent.tone` in place of the accent chosen in Settings. The floating capsule, terminal, file viewer and browser do not get the image.
- **Navigation** (`app/ui/MainActivity.kt`, `app/ui/Navigation.kt`): the conversation page is the root page, and the other pages (settings, models, memory and skills, scheduled tasks, browser, run process, sources, the phone-operation guide, the onboarding welcome and model configuration) are pushed onto the page stack `NavStack`; the back key and the pages' back buttons both return to the previous page (one entered from the sidebar returns to the sidebar that is open, one entered from Settings returns to Settings). Pages only get in and out through callbacks.
- **Sidebar** (`app/chat/ui/ChatDrawer.kt`): the menu at the top left of the conversation page slides out from the left. At the top are the logo, Claire and the readiness status; the entries are new conversation, scheduled tasks, memory and skills, browser and settings; below them are In progress (the current conversation and other conversations with a running task, which can be switched to when running in parallel; to the right of the section title, "Background screens ›" goes to the background screens page `app/chat/ui/ScreensScreen.kt`: one card per running task, saying what it is doing, which App it is using (the topmost App is re-checked through the already connected built-in assistant only when the task does something, not by polling) and its status; tapping a card returns to its conversation, and "View screen" expands a live screen inside the card (subscribed only while expanded, reusing the capsule's rate limit); tapping the screen enlarges it to the whole screen, with pinch zoom; screen numbers, pixels and other developer-only things are not written; the data comes from each task's own stream, so screens that are not being displayed are still visible when running in parallel), Pinned (pinned conversations, most recently pinned first) and Recent conversations (`drawerSections`); each conversation is listed in exactly one section: a pinned conversation with a running task is listed under In progress (carrying its pin too), and returns to Pinned when it finishes. When another conversation has a running task there is a small dot on the menu button. Long-pressing a saved conversation (with a haptic tick; in accessibility it is "Conversation actions") pops up a menu in place (`LongPressMenu()` in `app/chat/ui/ConversationMenu.kt`, also used for long-pressing a message on the conversation page): the whole screen darkens, and from Android 12 the conversation page and sidebar behind it also blur (earlier systems only darken); that row "lifts" in place into a slightly enlarged white card with a shadow (with the same content as the row in the sidebar), followed by a narrower white card aligned to its left edge, holding, in order, Pin (Unpin when already pinned), Rename and Delete in red, with the text on the left and the icon on the right, items separated by thin lines. If there is no room below (the row is near the bottom, or the keyboard is up) it goes above the row, and if there is no room on either side the row is moved up and the menu attached below it, never going outside the status bar, navigation bar or keyboard (`placeMenu`). Tapping outside or pressing back dismisses it (playing the appearance animation in reverse); with animations off, appearing and dismissing both go straight to the final state, without scaling. While the menu is open, the content behind it is not exposed to accessibility; the menu carries the panel title "Conversation actions" and a "Collapse" action, and each item is focusable and reads out its name. The menu is drawn in the same layer as the conversation page, covering the whole screen, positioned from the window coordinates of the long-pressed row. A new conversation not yet saved to the list does not respond to a long press. **Rename** pops up an input field containing the current title (all selected), saving after trimming both ends, and an empty one cannot be saved; it is written to `.title` (`ConversationStore.rename`, replaced whether or not there is already a title). A generated title is only written when there is no title yet (`setTitle`), so a background title arriving late cannot overwrite a name the user changed; the sidebar and the "Continue "…"" heading at the top of the floating capsule's card all switch to the new name following `ConversationStore.changes`. **Pin** writes a `.pin` (the pin time, `ConversationStore.setPinned`), and unpinning deletes it; the conversation list carries the pin time (`ConversationSummary.pinnedAt`) without changing the conversation's modified time, and after unpinning it returns to its old position under Recent; a pinned title has a small pin in front of it. A deleted conversation is no longer renamed or pinned. **Delete** pops up a confirmation "Delete this conversation?", stating the conversation title; if it has a task running or queued it also says that will be stopped, and if scheduled tasks are bound to it it says those entries will be kept; Delete in red on the right, Cancel on the left (the same look as confirmations for deleting things elsewhere). When conversations cannot be switched (a task in progress with parallel off) it cannot be opened, but a long press still works (a note to that effect is written above the conversations saved in the first section). After confirmation the sidebar stops listing it immediately (`ChatViewModel.deleted`); if the one deleted is the conversation on screen it is replaced with a new empty conversation and no longer follows its tasks; the rest is done in the background by `ConversationDeleter` (`app/chat/ConversationDeleter.kt`): scheduled tasks are not deleted (they have their own management page, and the next trigger leaves its result again under the original conversation id); first `TaskService.cancel` stops all its running and queued tasks and waits for them to wrap up (at most 15 seconds, and the "stopped" turn stored during the wrap-up is in the deletion's scope too), then its workspace is deleted (attachments in `inbox/`, the screens the user sent in `screens/`, files written by tasks; deleted by handle without following links, through `SandboxDirs.deleteWorkspace` holding the tree lock's exclusive permit, mutually exclusive with commands, imports and resets; a conversation that never had a workspace is skipped directly without taking the permit, and taking the exclusive permit has to reclaim the leases of terminals and stdio MCP servers opened by other conversations; this check is outside the permit, so writes that are creating a workspace are not waited for, and what gets created is left in "Used space" and listed as "Deleted conversations"), and finally `ConversationStore.delete` deletes the conversation file, `.title`, `.pin`, half-written temp files and corrupt files moved aside. Later writes that arrive after it (a title finished in the background, an outcome and account added after wrap-up) do not write it back; if someone stores another turn under this id it counts as starting over, and the sidebar lists it again. Run records (run process, run statistics) are global and are not deleted; files exported to the download directory belong to the user and are not deleted.
- **Conversation drafts**: the conversation page's text, attachments waiting to be sent, quote and steer/queue choice are kept per conversation id by `ChatViewModel`, saved and restored when switching conversations. A non-empty draft that has not yet sent its first sentence is also listed in the sidebar, and merely opening a draft does not run a task. A successful send clears this input, while a blocked send or an attachment send that was not accepted keeps it; when an attachment description comes back asynchronously it is accepted under the original conversation id and is not written into the conversation switched to meanwhile. The content URIs of ordinary attachments are kept only for the current process; text is restored using Android saved state. System sharing supports a single file (`ACTION_SEND`) and multiple files (`ACTION_SEND_MULTIPLE`, at most 9 at a time, truncated over the limit with a hint above the input field, and when some files fail to copy the successful ones are kept and each failure is reported); nothing is sent automatically: on a cold start it enters a new conversation, and when the UI is still alive in the background it enters the currently open conversation, and if that conversation already has an unsent draft it is first held as `ChatUi.pendingSharedDraft` for the user to choose "Replace draft" or "Cancel" (a pending share is also persisted by `SharedDraftStore`, so the dialog can still be restored after the process is reclaimed). Private attachment copies and share metadata are saved per conversation by `SharedDraftStore`, which can read the original single draft and the old single-attachment format; the corresponding copies are only cleaned up when sending, removing an attachment one by one, or deleting the conversation they belong to.
- **Conversation page** (`app/chat/ui/ChatScreen.kt`, with text in `app/chat/ui/ChatCopy.kt`): the top bar holds "Claire" and what it is doing right now (which App it is operating, or that it is waiting for you), and at the top right "⋯" (`ChatMoreMenu.kt`): new conversation, call records (tapping one jumps to the start of that call), things in this conversation (everything the front desk has dispatched, newest first, tapping one opens its run record, or its conversation if there is none) and call settings. An empty conversation is a greeting for the time of day and a column of examples, and tapping one sends it directly. While a task is in progress there is a status line (spinner, current progress, elapsed time) plus a vertical line of steps (a step with a concrete action has a small arrow on the right; tapping it lists what this step did one by one under the row: the UI's wording for the action · object · result, the same wording as the run process page, `Exchange.stepActions`, `ChatCopy.actionLine`; tool names and error codes are not exposed), with a line of small text underneath while it is operating the phone in the background; when it needs you (takeover, a question, a confirmation, paused, screen locked, blocked) progress stops at the current step (orange dot, "Paused"), and a white card below spells out what you need to do, with buttons. When finished, the process collapses into one line "Did N steps · took · View process" (into the timeline), and the answer is laid out as Markdown on a very light gray background (bottom-left tail corner, symmetric with the user's blue bubble), with a "Sources" capsule below (tap to see the original text and screens) and file cards. Long-pressing a message (a finished answer, a reply already finished before an interjection, the account added after stopping, or the user's own blue bubble; with a haptic tick, and in accessibility it is "Message actions") pops up the same in-place menu as the sidebar (`LongPressMenu()`, see the sidebar bullet above; the conversation page's part is in `app/chat/ui/MessageMenu.kt`): what is behind darkens (and from Android 12 also blurs), and that message lifts in place with a shadow (an answer is a white card, the user's words stay a blue bubble; a very long answer only lifts its first half screen), with Copy, Quote and Select text below, aligned to the right for the user's words, and so is the menu. **Copy** puts the whole message's plain text on the clipboard and shows "Copied": for an answer, Markdown symbols are removed (`MarkdownModel.toPlainText`: paragraphs, headings and code stay as they are, each list item on its own line with "• " or "1. ", quotation lines with "> ", table cells separated by tabs, and separators dropped), and for the user's words the attachment, screen and quote lines are removed; the "could not be verified" hint above, the sources capsule, file cards, photo grids and view process do not count. **Select text** turns that message back into a `SelectionContainer` (with a hint "Long-press the text to select it"): long-pressing again gives the system's selection handles and copy bar, and only a passage can be selected; it stays this way until the conversation is switched, while other messages still pop up the menu as usual. An answer that is still growing, or the passage interrupted by an interjection, does not pop up the menu and cannot be selected. The selection handles are in a separate popup window, so dragging a handle does not pull out the sidebar; after a long press, the subsequent drag-selection movement is consumed by the selection, and the sidebar's horizontal swipe and the list's vertical scroll do not move with it, while swipes and scrolls without a long press work as before. Links in the answer still open as usual. **Quote** puts that message's plain text into a small strip above the input field (a blue line on the left, at most two lines, "× Remove quote" on the right, `ChatUi.quote`), which goes out with the next sentence and disappears after sending; when switching conversations it is saved with that conversation's draft and restored when switching back; a quote with no words is not sent (it is fine with an attachment). The quote is written at the very front of the user's sentence (`core/agent/QuoteMessage.kt`): the first line says whose words are quoted, `> [Quoting your earlier reply]` (Claire's answer) or `> [Quoting my earlier message]` (the user's own words), then each line starting with "> " is the quoted content (at most 1000 characters, with the rest cut off and "…" added), and after a blank line comes what is said this time, with attachment lines still attached at the end. This sentence is stored as it is in `Turn.userText` (interjections in `Turn.steers`) and handed to the task, with no new fields: a new task, an interjection (while a task is running) and an answer to a question are all the same, the model sees directly what is quoted, and it is also carried in the context of earlier turns; messages in old conversations do not have that line and are displayed as they are. The conversation page draws the quoted message as a small pale block above the bubble (at most two lines), and the conversation title, sidebar and notifications refer to this sentence without the quote (`ChatCopy.plainTitle`, `ScreenCopy.plain`). The floating capsule's card does none of this: it lives in an accessibility overlay window and normally does not take focus, so selection handles and the system copy bar either cannot appear there or are unreliable; the sentence at its top naming the task likewise carries no quote. The input bar is a glass capsule, and you can still type while a task is running, as an interjection (with gray placeholder text). The turns on screen are those stored by `ConversationStore`: whichever conversation it wrote, it emits that conversation's id in `ConversationStore.changes`, no matter who wrote it (here, the floating capsule, a scheduled task); `ChatViewModel` re-reads when it receives the id of the conversation on screen, merging by run id with the turns already followed here (using this side's copy, with steps and elapsed time, without duplicates), and the question-and-answer currently being followed stays at the end; when the sidebar is open it also re-reads the conversation list with it. When the conversation page becomes visible again (returning to the foreground, coming back from another page, `ChatViewModel.refresh`) it fills in once more and attaches tasks in this conversation that were started elsewhere and are not being followed here (using the task's own words, and interjections made elsewhere are attached too); without waiting to return to the foreground, a task started elsewhere in the conversation on screen is attached as soon as it has a state, so while it runs its words and steps are there. The service registers a task in the task list (`publishRuns`) before showing it (`TaskRun.show`), so the words are already available when the UI receives the state; if they are not yet available when attaching, they are filled in as soon as the task list updates. When the parallel switch is off, the UI also identifies by the conversation id carried in the state and does not attach tasks from other conversations to the conversation on screen; when the service switches to showing a just-started task in another conversation, the service is made to show back the task being followed here.
- **Replies taken by the front desk in the conversation** (`app/chat/ui/DeskExchange.kt`, `DeskTray.kt`, `DeskNeedCard.kt`, `ChatTimeMark.kt`): the several replies under one sentence (what the front desk promised, a finished result, an old-style answer) are all the same light gray block (`colors.answer` + `MikaShapes.AnswerBubble`), full Markdown, hugging short text and filling the width for long text, 8dp apart; a reply does not say "Claire · time · tokens" above it, there are no buttons below, and the long-press menu is as before. When two adjacent turns are more than 5 minutes apart, a line with the time goes between them (today "14:02", yesterday "Yesterday 21:30", earlier with the date; not drawn separately when it sits right next to a call start or end line). Before the front desk has spoken there is a "Thinking" block with three bouncing dots. A finished result's original text is written as a one-line report, not paraphrased by the front desk model and not truncated; for a failure the front desk states the reason and the next step. Work in progress does not put cards into the conversation: above the input bar there is a glass progress strip (`DeskTray`), one line when collapsed (status dot, name, the current step, elapsed time, and how many more `+N`), and when expanded one paragraph per item (name, stage, the original words handed to the background, a vertical line of steps with each step's time, and "Stop" and "View detailed process ›" at the bottom); for each item that needs you to choose, answer or take over/confirm there is a question card at the end of the conversation (`NeedCard()`), and choosing an option answers that item directly; for takeover, allow once, or a task you must do first, the card draws the same buttons as the chat page from that item's current state (`ChatReduce.needStatus`, `NeedStatusCard()`), and through `deskNeedOps` acts only on that item without jumping to its conversation; when the state cannot be read for the moment, only "Stop this item" is offered. The only process the user sees is the run record on the "View detailed process" page; the child conversation the item runs in is not exposed. When the conversation itself has no task, the small screen follows what the front desk has dispatched: among the running ones that have a screen, the one needing you, then the most recently touched one is chosen and the service is made to show it; with more than one running, the bottom left of the small window says which one it is. Enlarging and closing are remembered per item. When the page is gone (`DeskNotifier`), starting, progress and needing your attention do not write lines into the conversation, only notifications; finishing writes the original text, and a failure writes one line with the reason. The call page has no "⋯"; call records and call settings are entered from here.
- **Only Claire is visible to the user**: front desk and background are internal divisions that the user cannot see and should not hear about. The words the model can read (`front_desk.md`, `front_desk_phone.md`, `LlmBrain.BACKGROUND_PREFIX`, `DeskTools` descriptions, and the front desk turns in `Conversation.context`) mention only Claire; the prompts require Claire to speak about what it is doing in the first person, without mentioning a front desk, background, dispatch, task board or task id; `OneMikaWordingTest` guards this. On each reseed of history (`LlmBrain.seed`) the latest thing Claire said keeps its full text (up to `DESK_SEED_LATEST_MAX_CHARS`), so a follow-up on a long result just written into the conversation can be answered; earlier ones are clipped per `DESK_RESULT_MAX_CHARS`. Tool results for realtime voice are limited by protocol frame length and are still given per `DESK_RESULT_MAX_CHARS`.
- **Conversation branching** (stored as `branch` / `branchBefore` in `app/chat/ConversationStore.kt`, workspace copying in `app/chat/ConversationBrancher.kt`, the decision in `app/chat/ui/ChatBranch.kt`): long-pressing an answer that has already finished (success, failure and stopped all count as finished) adds a "Branch from here" item to the menu; long-pressing that turn's own words from the user adds an "Ask again from here" item. Whether the items are shown and whether the action runs after tapping are the same decision (`canBranchAt` / `canRetryAt`, given to the menu via `ChatViewModel.branchTurn` / `retryTurn`): the turn has finished, the conversation has been saved, no task is running or queued, nothing is being deleted, and the input field is not busy; asking again also requires that sentence to be non-empty. **Branch from here** copies the turns from the source conversation up to and including this turn into a new conversation; **Ask again from here** copies only the turns before this turn (there are no earlier turns before the first turn, a prefilled non-empty draft can be opened from the sidebar, and it counts as the first turn only once the first sentence is sent), strips web notes, quotes, attachment lines and screen lines from the original words and prefills them into the input field without sending automatically; when this sentence or sentences from earlier turns carry attachment lines, the files in the source conversation's `inbox/` are copied one by one into the new conversation's `inbox/` (this sentence's attachments first, then those written in earlier turns, then the rest, all under the same limits below; `AndroidBranchWorkspace.copyInboxForRetry`), each copied on its own with its own success or failure, not through a temp directory; anything over the total limit (200 MB), whose source file is gone, or that fails to copy is skipped. Copies made from this sentence's attachments are put back into the input field; ones written in this sentence and earlier turns that were not copied are listed as a hint, while the rest that were not copied give no hint. Before sending, the files put back are checked once more to be readable (`checkInboxAttachments`); if any one cannot be read, this send does not happen and those attachments are listed as not accepted. When no attachment lines are involved at all, the workspace is copied whole the way branching does below. Screens are not carried along, and the user is prompted to add them again. The new conversation's title is the source title (or its first sentence if there is none) plus the localized suffix " · Branch"; when the total length is exceeded the source title is truncated and the suffix kept, truncated by code point so half an emoji is never cut off (`ChatCopy.branchTitle`); a web source `.page` is copied as it is. Which conversation and which turn it branched from, and whether this turn is included, are recorded in a `.branch` file next to the conversation file (`ConversationStore.branchOrigin`, JSON: parent, turn, after, at; a temp file is written first and then renamed; deleted together with the conversation), and such conversations have a small branch icon in front of their title in the sidebar (`DrawerConversation.branch`). If the source file cannot be read, the turn is out of range, or the target already has a conversation file, nothing is written and nothing is switched.
